# MWF 0.6.2 requirement audit

Christopher's [one-model scope change](single-model-scope.md) cancels legacy
compatibility, embedded old-state migration/import/backfill, dual old/new
execution modes, and compatibility-only adapters, tests, and reviews. Those
obligations are cancelled rather than pending blockers. Historical results
remain recorded. Native 0.6.2 functionality remains required. This task writes
the external `migration.md` guide at the end from the completed implementation.

Christopher settled [Q10](architectural-questions.md#q10-dynamic-component-discovery-during-admitted-execution)
on 2026-09-07. Component merging during an admitted run is unsupported and
assumed not to occur; it is no longer an unanswered architecture blocker.
Neither live merging nor retention and deferred reconciliation of such runtime
discoveries is required for 0.6.2. The node-scoped, explicitly initialized
programmatic autostart redesign is deferred to approximately 0.6.4. Existing
between-run membership-change rules and historical producing identity remain
required. This scope decision does not itself earn implementation credit.

The [current native workflow-management batch](native-workflow-management-progress.md)
adds interrupt execution, applied recovery, thread ownership, project-wide API
capacity, clipboard recovery, component observation, and compact lineage.
Broader checks and review corrections remain active. This update records scope;
it grants no whole-requirement or final acceptance credit.

Status: the local implementation checkpoint is verified; final runtime and issue acceptance remain open.

The earlier batch summary above and increment summaries below preserve the state recorded at those points and may describe work as unfinished then. For present status, use the [current implementation reconciliation](#current-implementation-reconciliation) and the four current cells in each requirement row. This historical framing does not supersede the settled requirement, scope, dependency, or other source fields in each row's first five cells.

The [native preview and sampling increment](native-preview-sampling-progress.md)
passed all 1,833 checks across 60 complete modules. It covers the existing
read-only command paths, native sample admission and execution, filtered replay,
interrupted-admission cleanup, and retained-history sampled resume. The linked
record preserves earlier failures and bounded review closures. Full nine-command
planning, interrupts, applied recovery, native cleanup, and final acceptance
remain unfinished. No whole requirement receives completion credit.

The [nine graph commands increment](nine-command-progress.md) adds shared
selection, full graph previews, interval execution, and reset refusal before
project loading. Its final combined run passed 2,017 checks across 78 complete
modules. Its repeated API benchmark validated 4,320 executions. The linked
progress record states the acceptance condition for documentation review and
source-to-commit checks.
The [between-run membership increment](between-run-membership-progress.md)
adds regional active membership, immutable producing identities, explicit
repair for reusable history, and automatic reconciliation without reusable
work. Its final broad run passed all 2,167 checks across 93 complete modules,
with no failures, errors, or skips. The repeated API benchmark validated 4,320
executions. The linked record preserves earlier failures and states the
remaining documentation and source-to-commit acceptance conditions. Interrupts,
applied recovery, clipboard atomicity, native cleanup, and final issue acceptance
remain unfinished.

The [guarded queued component start](ordinary-component-start.md) is an accepted
private storage increment. It preserves exact component state and
ownership through one transition. Shared public lifecycle activation remains
unfinished.

The [component result and session-exit increment](component-session-settlement.md)
is accepted for private terminal arbitration and guarded batch publication.
Its focused checks and two independent Sol reviews passed. The mixed adjacent
result retains its documented failures. It completes no whole requirement.

The [consistent component-state reader](component-state-observations.md) is
accepted as a private prerequisite for parent readiness. It reads one snapshot
and preserves caller transactions. Parent discovery, complete parent selection,
and runtime admission remain unfinished.

The [success publication within a live session](component-success-publication.md)
is accepted privately. It preserves session scope while publishing one completed
component. Shared caller activation and parent scheduling remain unfinished.

The [native public component progress](native-public-component-progress.md)
records ordinary activation and nested-repair increments. The same-component
ordinary-remainder correction and its regression cases remain included. Its
final source passed all 609 checks across 15 full surrounding modules, plus
57 focused checks. CLI integration and wider lifecycle requirements remain
unfinished; this bounded local change completes no whole requirement row.

The [producing execution identity progress](execution-producing-identity-progress.md)
records captured shape/alignment, durable job creator ancestry, exact selected
root claims, and accepted child restart continuation. Integrated checks passed
860 cases, and 181 additional affected-path cases passed on the final source.
Both bounded Sol reviews returned PASS, as recorded in that progress document. Automatic ordinary child execution, full
selected-job lifecycle, and the remaining managed-file integration remain unfinished;
no whole requirement receives completion credit from this increment.

The [managed input ownership progress](managed-input-ownership-progress.md)
records immutable producing executions, ambiguous path claimants, synchronous
batch restoration, and coherent ownership reads. The final 22-module selection
passed all 774 checks, including the corrected native controls. Both independent
reviews returned bounded PASS. Receiver causes, cleanup, guards, and crash
recovery remain pending. The same change includes
[automatic programmatic fresh preparation](programmatic-fresh-preparation.md),
with native parent preflight and exact admitted selection checks. No whole
requirement receives completion credit from this increment.

The [receiver input misalignment increment](receiver-input-misalignment-progress.md)
adds atomic flags and first causes for managed files, validated current cause
reads, and a cache tied to committed result identity. The final 28-module selection passed all 1,032 checks. Both independent
reviews returned bounded source PASS.
That increment did not include job-arrival causes. Preparation causes and trace
integration remain unfinished; the increment completes no whole requirement.

The [managed job arrival increment](managed-job-arrivals-progress.md) extends
receiver flags and first causes to job creation, including publication failures
and current-shape checks. Its final 30-module repeat passed all 1,116 cases,
including all six corrected adjacent failures. Both source reviews returned
bounded PASS. Final acceptance requires the linked documentation and commit
checks; this increment completes no whole requirement.

The [producer footprint preparation increment](producer-footprint-preparation-progress.md)
adds complete preflight, excluded receiver guards, immutable ownership cleanup,
durable preparation receipts, and removal causes to existing full run and reset
paths. Native resume adds whole-selection readiness and owner preflight, atomic
output recovery and requeue, and retained timing and terminal history. The final
44-module repeat passed all 1,347 cases; the three-process benchmark passed
4,320 job executions. The linked increment records its independent reviews and
source-to-commit verification. Selected-job and interval integration remain
unfinished. This increment completes no whole requirement.

The [selected execution increment](selected-execution-progress.md) adds exact
root preparation, newly created same-component causal execution, finite joined
execution rounds, retained nested repairs, and sampled/done settlement with
successful history. Native restart preserves exact owners and generation
fences. Its complete surrounding repeat passed 1,638 cases; the final added
settlement regression passed with its full module and module-size check, 64
cases. The repeated-use benchmark passed 4,320 executions. Random sampling,
interval commands, sampled resume, interruption, read-only planning, recovery,
and final issue-wide verification remain unfinished. No whole requirement row
receives completion credit from this increment.

The [interval calculation](stage-s1a.md),
[command retirement](stage-retirement.md), [shared topology](stage-topology.md),
[retained scheduler repair](stage-scheduler-completion.md),
[private session storage](stage-session-foundation.md),
[checkpoint renewal recorded with legacy loading](stage-legacy-loading.md),
[retained deadlines](stage-retained-deadlines.md),
[private component ownership](stage-component-ownership.md),
[private component-state storage](stage-component-state-storage.md),
[private sampled resume](stage-sampled-component-resume.md),
[private component terminal storage](stage-component-terminal-storage.md),
[pure sampling calculation](stage-sample-calculations.md),
[pure component readiness](stage-component-readiness.md),
[repeated API verification](stage-repeated-api-verification.md), and
[aggregate API-limit deprecation](stage-api-total-deprecation.md) are accepted
within their recorded boundaries. Public integration, other stages, and final
review remain pending.

The earlier [legacy migration preflight](stage-migration-guard.md) and the
legacy-loading portions of [legacy loading and checkpoint renewal](stage-legacy-loading.md)
are historical under the one-model scope. Their exact outcomes remain linked,
but they are no longer supported runtime behavior or final release gates.
The checkpoint renewal behavior in the mixed stage remains native and accepted
within its recorded boundary.

[Architectural decisions](architectural-questions.md) preserve the source
investigations and record Christopher's approved answers to AQ1 through AQ6 and
Q7 through Q9. The exact supplemental approval is the
[2026-09-05 architecture decision record](https://github.com/Christopher8187/product/issues/45#issuecomment-5552346343),
matching Parent Repo
`testing_ground/issue-45/approved-architecture-decisions-20260905.md` at SHA-256

None of those nine questions remains open within its stated scope. Their

AQ2, AQ4, and Q7 are historical only; AQ1 and AQ3 retain their native portions;
AQ5, AQ6, Q8, and Q9 remain fully applicable.

Authoritative behavior source: [Settle the MWF workflow-management model for 0.6.2, final consolidated resolution](https://github.com/Christopher8187/product/issues/44#issuecomment-5539997969), abbreviated `44-FCR` below. The still-active architecture gate is [Settle the MWF workflow-management model for 0.6.2, cross-session architecture clarification](https://github.com/Christopher8187/product/issues/44#issuecomment-5463708247), abbreviated `44-ARCH`. The authoritative work procedure is [Implement and verify the agreed MWF 0.6.2 workflow-management changes](https://github.com/Christopher8187/product/issues/45), abbreviated `45-PROC`. Source applicability and supersession are recorded in [source-applicability.md](./source-applicability.md).

The proposed stages are:

- `S1`: selection and read-only paths.
- `S2`: component and session storage, native persistence, reservations,
  ownership, and recovery foundations.
- `S3`: publication ownership, fresh preparation, guards, misalignment, and membership repair.
- `S4`: ordinary commands, readiness, sampling, and causal selected work.
- `S5`: interrupt boundaries, explicit interruption, transfers, holds, and ownership-dependent controls.
- `S6`: lineage, diagnostics, CLI and documentation reconciliation, skills, and integrated verification.
- `ALL`: applies to every relevant stage.
- `GATE`: must hold before an affected stage can be accepted.
- `FINAL`: applies only after every readiness condition for the final review is met.

Every implementation, verification, stage-review, and disposition field began as `pending`. Updating a field requires a link or exact local reference to the corresponding change, command result, review, or decision.

in progress. Its 23 cases pass with all 66 identity and 78 bootstrap cases,
167 total in the recorded combined run. It connects ordinary CLI execution to
native ownership, heartbeats, and terminal cleanup. The separate
[programmatic execution group](stage-native-programmatic-execution.md) now
connects public entry points and task-side reuse to that shared lifecycle.

combined native run passes 214 checks, including all 47 programmatic cases.

and adds 18 focused checks. Its broader selection passes 389 checks, and its

## Performance priority

On 2026-09-05 Christopher said, "Don't worry too much about the performance.
We're adding functionality, so some performance hits may be expected."
Applying that instruction, correctness and completion take priority. Modest
measured slowdowns are reported rather than used as standalone blockers.
Additional timing comparisons are not required solely to resolve such costs.
Hangs, unbounded delays, lost data, and inconsistent state remain correctness
concerns. Historical timing failures retain their original results and limits;
this instruction does not turn them into passes or authorize threshold changes.

## Combined functional selection

The exact 298-file `sample-calculations-combined-functionality-01` freeze has
SHA-256
`F688AC7E0B395AAC607D17B411145C543E63852D7E5ADD6DE6198E12E30412FF`.
Its ordinary suite passed 884 cases with one marked stress case deselected in
581.88 seconds. Four fresh-process cyclic cases passed in 3.24, 7.33, 7.60,
and 3.22 seconds, and the separately selected marked stress case passed in
6.84 seconds. All six manifests contain the same 298 source hashes, and every
JUnit result reports zero failures, errors, and skips.

The independent
[combined functionality acceptance review](../../../../testing_ground/issue-45/combined-functionality-acceptance-review.md)
records a bounded Sol xhigh PASS. It covers the interaction of retained runtime
corrections, private ownership, pure calculations, repeated API verification,
and the intended aggregate API-limit warnings. It does not activate the private
modules or accept sampling integration, component lifecycle, the newer 300-file
component-state work, release, the whole issue, or final Astra review.

## Current audit state

Earlier stage records retain their bounded acceptance and historical failures.
The [current integration record](native-workflow-management-progress.md) owns
the latest batch's implementation, checks, and unresolved findings. Detailed
requirement rows still need reconciliation against that resulting code and its
completed reviews. This record does not accept the whole issue, release, or
final Astra review.
